flat_cut\n\n    # Remove the bore from the knob body\n    knob = knob - bore\n\n    # Add a grip ring at mid-height\n    # This provides good grip surface and increases minimum width at mid-height\n    grip_ring_height = 4\n    grip_ring_diameter = 40\n    grip_ring = Cylinder(\n        radius=grip_ring_diameter / 2,\n        height=grip_ring_height,\n        align=(Align.CENTER, Align.CENTER, Align.MIN),\n    )\n    grip_ring = grip_ring.locate(Location(0, 0, knob_height / 2 - flat_cut\n\n    # Remove bore from body\n    knob = body - bore\n\n    # Add support ring at top of bore to eliminate overhang\n    # This is a ring at the top that supports the bore opening\n    support_ring_h = 0.8\n    support_ring_d = bore_diameter + 0.5\n    support_ring = Cylinder(\n        radius=support_ring_d / 2, height=support_ring_h\n    ).translate((0, 0, knob_height - (bore_flat_width / 2) ** 2))\n\n    # Flat cut box\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        width=bore_flat_width + 0.5,\n        length=flat_depth + 0.3,\n        height=knob_height + 2,\n        align=(Align.CENTER, Align.MIN, Align.MIN),\n    )\n\n    # D-shaped bore\n    bore = bore_cyl - flat_cut\n\n    # Remove bore from body\n    knob = body - bore\n\n    # Add chamfer to bore opening to support overhang\n    bore_opening_edges = [e for e in knob.edges() if abs(e.center.z - knob_height) < 0.5]\n    if bore_opening_edges:\n        knob = chamfer(bore_opening_edges, length=0.5)\n\n    # Add grip ring at mid-height with smooth transitions\n    grip_h = 3\n    grip_d_mid = 36\n    grip_z = knob_height / 2 - (bore_flat_width / 2) ** 2))\n\n    # Flat cut\n    flat_cut = Box(\n        width=bore_flat_width + 0.5,\n        length=flat_depth + 0.3,\n        height=knob_height + 2,\n        align=(Align.CENTER, Align.MIN, Align.MIN),\n    )\n\n    # D-shaped bore\n    bore = bore_cyl - flat_cut\n\n    # Remove bore from body\n    knob = body - bore\n\n    # Add base for print bed\n    base = Cylinder(radius=knob_base_d / 2 - 0.5, height=1.5)\n    knob = knob + base\n\n    # Polish edges\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 0.8)\n\n    return knob\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":202,"cache_read_input_tokens":60472,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":202},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"744a8a3f-b832-4fec-9127-2bc5158059a8","uuid":"54835bf4-0b6d-4a25-a726-81a7850bb729","timestamp":"2026-08-24T01:22:59.046Z","request_id":"req_011CeLhTYPEgQYHkLv27kbWk"}
